static void Equal<T>(T expected, T actual, string name)
{
    if (!EqualityComparer<T>.Default.Equals(expected, actual))
        throw new InvalidOperationException($"{name}: expected {expected}, got {actual}");
}

static void True(bool value, string name)
{
    if (!value) throw new InvalidOperationException($"{name}: expected true");
}

static void RunArmyRtsCommandContinuityRulesTests()
{
    IReadOnlyList<WarArmyAssignment> normalReplacements =
        KingdomWarDirectorRules.AllocateWars(
            new[]
            {
                new WarAllocationFacts(1L, capitalThreat: false,
                    warGoalThreat: true, signedWarScore: 0,
                    requiredArmies: 2)
            },
            new[]
            {
                new ArmyAllocationFacts(10L, 20),
                new ArmyAllocationFacts(11L, 20)
            });
    Equal(ArmyRtsRole.Assault, normalReplacements[0].Role,
        "a replacement army does not default to defense");
    Equal(ArmyRtsRole.Assault, normalReplacements[1].Role,
        "every operational replacement remains attack-capable without a homeland threat");

    IReadOnlyList<WarArmyAssignment> threatenedReplacements =
        KingdomWarDirectorRules.AllocateWars(
            new[]
            {
                new WarAllocationFacts(2L, capitalThreat: true,
                    warGoalThreat: true, signedWarScore: 0,
                    requiredArmies: 2, localTerritoryThreat: true)
            },
            new[]
            {
                new ArmyAllocationFacts(20L, 20),
                new ArmyAllocationFacts(21L, 20)
            });
    Equal(ArmyRtsRole.Defense, threatenedReplacements[0].Role,
        "a real capital threat reserves one defense slot");
    Equal(ArmyRtsRole.Assault, threatenedReplacements[1].Role,
        "additional replacements remain available to attack");
    Equal(ArmyRtsRole.Reserve,
        KingdomWarDirectorRules.ResolveMissionRole(
            ArmyRtsRole.Assault, hasStrategicTarget: false,
            forceReady: true),
        "an operational field army without an open target waits in reserve");
    Equal(true, KingdomWarDirectorRules.ShouldAllocateFieldArmy(
            unitCount: 20, captainAlive: true, royalGuard: false,
            dedicatedGarrison: false, specialArmy: false),
        "an ordinary former defense army can return to field allocation");
    Equal(false, KingdomWarDirectorRules.ShouldAllocateFieldArmy(
            unitCount: 20, captainAlive: true, royalGuard: false,
            dedicatedGarrison: false, specialArmy: true),
        "a special army never enters ordinary defense-to-attack conversion");
    True(KingdomWarDirectorRules.ShouldUsePriorityDefenseTarget(
            ArmyRtsObjectiveState.OpenDefense),
        "an active capital threat keeps the priority defense target");
    Equal(false, KingdomWarDirectorRules.ShouldUsePriorityDefenseTarget(
            ArmyRtsObjectiveState.ClosedOccupied),
        "a cleared capital threat falls through to another open objective");
    Equal(ArmyRtsRole.Assault,
        KingdomWarDirectorRules.ResolveMissionRole(
            ArmyRtsRole.Defense, hasStrategicTarget: true,
            forceReady: true, friendlyDefenseTarget: true,
            ArmyRtsProposalKind.Attack),
        "a defense army counterattacking an occupied homeland city receives an assault mission");
    Equal(ArmyRtsRole.Defense,
        KingdomWarDirectorRules.ResolveMissionRole(
            ArmyRtsRole.Assault, hasStrategicTarget: true,
            forceReady: true, friendlyDefenseTarget: true,
            ArmyRtsProposalKind.Defend),
        "an actively contested homeland city still receives a defense mission");

    True(ArmyRtsTaskOwnershipRules.HasImmediateCombatPriority(
            hasAttackTarget: true, targetAlive: true,
            targetHostile: true, targetCombatant: true,
            distanceSquared: 25d),
        "a live nearby hostile combatant may temporarily preempt RTS");
    Equal(false, ArmyRtsTaskOwnershipRules.HasImmediateCombatPriority(
            hasAttackTarget: false, targetAlive: true,
            targetHostile: true, targetCombatant: true,
            distanceSquared: 1d),
        "an in-combat task without an attack target cannot retain command");
    Equal(false, ArmyRtsTaskOwnershipRules.HasImmediateCombatPriority(
            hasAttackTarget: true, targetAlive: false,
            targetHostile: true, targetCombatant: true,
            distanceSquared: 1d),
        "a dead target cannot retain command");
    Equal(false, ArmyRtsTaskOwnershipRules.HasImmediateCombatPriority(
            hasAttackTarget: true, targetAlive: true,
            targetHostile: false, targetCombatant: true,
            distanceSquared: 1d),
        "a friendly target cannot retain command");
    Equal(false, ArmyRtsTaskOwnershipRules.HasImmediateCombatPriority(
            hasAttackTarget: true, targetAlive: true,
            targetHostile: true, targetCombatant: false,
            distanceSquared: 1d),
        "a non-combatant cannot retain command");
    Equal(false, ArmyRtsTaskOwnershipRules.HasImmediateCombatPriority(
            hasAttackTarget: true, targetAlive: true,
            targetHostile: true, targetCombatant: true,
            distanceSquared: 400d),
        "a distant combat target cannot indefinitely preempt RTS movement");

    Equal(ArmyRtsState.Rally, ArmyRtsRules.ResolveState(
            new ArmyRtsTransitionFacts
            {
                CurrentState = ArmyRtsState.Regroup,
                HasMission = true,
                Posture = ArmyRtsPosture.Retreat,
                MinimumForceReady = true,
                RegroupReady = false,
                Organization = 100,
                Supply = 100
            }),
        "a recovered retreat with minimum force must leave the safe-city regroup anchor");
    Equal(ArmyRtsState.Regroup, ArmyRtsRules.ResolveState(
            new ArmyRtsTransitionFacts
            {
                CurrentState = ArmyRtsState.Regroup,
                HasMission = true,
                Posture = ArmyRtsPosture.Automatic,
                MinimumForceReady = true,
                RegroupReady = false,
                Organization = 100,
                Supply = 100
            }),
        "a normal understrength regroup still waits for departure strength");
    Equal(ArmyRtsState.Regroup, ArmyRtsRules.ResolveState(
            new ArmyRtsTransitionFacts
            {
                CurrentState = ArmyRtsState.Regroup,
                HasMission = true,
                Posture = ArmyRtsPosture.Retreat,
                MinimumForceReady = false,
                RegroupReady = false,
                Organization = 100,
                Supply = 100
            }),
        "a retreat below minimum operational force remains in the safe city");
    Equal(false, ArmyRtsRules.HasDepartureStrength(
            living: 9, targetStrength: 42, minimumForceReady: true,
            replenishmentBypassActive: true),
        "a stalled replenishment request never authorizes an understrength Army to depart");
    Equal(false, ArmyRtsRules.CanCaptainAdvanceWithEscort(
            requiresEscort: true, rosterLiving: 10,
            nearbyFollowers: 0, captainPresent: true,
            immediateCombat: false, transportOwnsMovement: false),
        "a captain cannot march alone while the army remains behind");
    Equal(false, ArmyRtsRules.CanCaptainAdvanceWithEscort(
            requiresEscort: true, rosterLiving: 10,
            nearbyFollowers: 6, captainPresent: true,
            immediateCombat: false, transportOwnsMovement: false),
        "a captain waits while fewer than eighty percent are nearby");
    True(ArmyRtsRules.CanCaptainAdvanceWithEscort(
            requiresEscort: true, rosterLiving: 10,
            nearbyFollowers: 7, captainPresent: true,
            immediateCombat: false, transportOwnsMovement: false),
        "a captain advances once the eighty-percent escort quorum is present");
    Equal(false, ArmyRtsRules.CanCaptainAdvanceWithEscort(
            requiresEscort: true, rosterLiving: 1,
            nearbyFollowers: 0, captainPresent: true,
            immediateCombat: true, transportOwnsMovement: false),
        "an attack target cannot make an isolated captain leave his army");
    Equal(false, ArmyRtsRules.IsViableAttackAssignment(
            attackAssignment: true, warActive: true,
            objectiveState: ArmyRtsObjectiveState.OpenAttack,
            living: 7, targetStrength: 10, minimumOperationalForce: 2),
        "an understrength stale attack cannot block objective handoff");
    Equal(false, ArmyRtsRules.IsViableAttackAssignment(
            attackAssignment: true, warActive: true,
            objectiveState: ArmyRtsObjectiveState.ClosedOccupied,
            living: 10, targetStrength: 10, minimumOperationalForce: 2),
        "an occupied target cannot count as a continuing attack");
    True(ArmyRtsRules.IsViableAttackAssignment(
            attackAssignment: true, warActive: true,
            objectiveState: ArmyRtsObjectiveState.OpenAttack,
            living: 8, targetStrength: 10, minimumOperationalForce: 2),
        "an active open attack at eighty percent preserves continuity");
    True(DiplomaticWarDeclarationLedgerRules.MatchesDirectedWarPair(
            recordAttackerId: 10L, recordDefenderId: 20L,
            warAttackerId: 10L, warDefenderId: 20L),
        "ending A-B clears A's declaration against B");
    Equal(false,
        DiplomaticWarDeclarationLedgerRules.MatchesDirectedWarPair(
            recordAttackerId: 10L, recordDefenderId: 30L,
            warAttackerId: 10L, warDefenderId: 20L),
        "ending A-B preserves A's pending declaration against C");
    Equal(false,
        DiplomaticWarDeclarationLedgerRules.MatchesDirectedWarPair(
            recordAttackerId: 20L, recordDefenderId: 10L,
            warAttackerId: 10L, warDefenderId: 20L),
        "the reverse declaration is handled explicitly for its owner");
    True(ArmyRtsRules.ShouldRemainInReplenishment(
            needsReplenishment: true, departureStrengthReady: false),
        "an understrength Army remains in replenishment after its retry window expires");
    Equal(33, TemporaryLevyRules.MergeDirectedReplenishmentDemand(
            currentDemand: 33, currentMissingStrength: 33),
        "repeating an unchanged directed replenishment request does not double its demand");
    True(ArmyRtsRules.ShouldHandoffObjective(
            nextState: ArmyRtsState.Idle, targetComplete: false,
            targetValid: false),
        "a destroyed mission city hands its Army back to the war director");

    var flow = new ArmyWatchdogRecoveryFlow();
    flow.AssignMission();
    Equal(ArmyStallRecoveryAction.None,
        flow.ObserveSample(true, ArmyWatchdogPositionSource.Captain,
            0d, 0, routeReady: false, routePending: false,
            commandExpected: true, commandOwned: false),
        "the first missing-command sample remains bounded");
    Equal(ArmyStallRecoveryAction.None,
        flow.ObserveSample(true, ArmyWatchdogPositionSource.Captain,
            0d, 0, routeReady: false, routePending: false,
            commandExpected: true, commandOwned: false),
        "the second missing-command sample remains bounded");
    Equal(ArmyStallRecoveryAction.ReassertCommand,
        flow.ObserveSample(true, ArmyWatchdogPositionSource.Captain,
            0d, 0, routeReady: false, routePending: false,
            commandExpected: true, commandOwned: false),
        "three stationary assault samples reassert command before replanning");
    flow.ObserveSample(true, ArmyWatchdogPositionSource.Captain,
        0d, 0, routeReady: false, routePending: false,
        commandExpected: true, commandOwned: true);
    flow.ObserveSample(true, ArmyWatchdogPositionSource.Captain,
        0d, 0, routeReady: false, routePending: false,
        commandExpected: true, commandOwned: true);
    Equal(ArmyStallRecoveryAction.RebuildRoute,
        flow.ObserveSample(true, ArmyWatchdogPositionSource.Captain,
            0d, 0, routeReady: false, routePending: false,
            commandExpected: true, commandOwned: true),
        "a second stationary window replans after command reassertion");
    Equal(false, ArmyStallWatchdogRules.ShouldUseAlternateEndpoint(
            ArmyStallRecoveryAction.RebuildRoute,
            commandExpected: true, commandOwned: true),
        "the first command-owned physical stall rebuilds the current target");
    Equal(false, ArmyStallWatchdogRules.ShouldUseAlternateEndpoint(
            ArmyStallRecoveryAction.RebuildRoute,
            commandExpected: false, commandOwned: false),
        "an asynchronous planning stall first rebuilds the same route");
    True(ArmyStallWatchdogRules.ShouldUseAlternateEndpoint(
            ArmyStallRecoveryAction.AlternateEndpoint,
            commandExpected: false, commandOwned: false),
        "the explicit alternate recovery always changes endpoint");

    Equal(ArmyWatchdogPositionSource.Captain,
        ArmyStallWatchdogRules.SelectMissionPositionSource(
            captainAvailable: true, formationAnchorAvailable: true,
            formationMemberAvailable: true,
            formationProgressExpected: false),
        "a marching Army observes its captain even when one follower is stranded");
    Equal(ArmyWatchdogPositionSource.FormationMember,
        ArmyStallWatchdogRules.SelectMissionPositionSource(
            captainAvailable: true, formationAnchorAvailable: true,
            formationMemberAvailable: true,
            formationProgressExpected: true),
        "deployment observes the member that has not reached its formation slot");
    True(ArmyStallWatchdogRules.ShouldUseMemberRecovery(
            ArmyWatchdogPositionSource.FormationMember,
            ArmyStallRecoveryAction.RebuildRoute,
            objectiveOpen: true),
        "a stranded member rebuild request remains member scoped");
    Equal(false, ArmyStallWatchdogRules.ShouldUseMemberRecovery(
            ArmyWatchdogPositionSource.Captain,
            ArmyStallRecoveryAction.RebuildRoute,
            objectiveOpen: true),
        "a stalled captain may rebuild the strategic Army route");
    Equal(false, ArmyStallWatchdogRules.ShouldUseMemberRecovery(
            ArmyWatchdogPositionSource.FormationMember,
            ArmyStallRecoveryAction.ChangeTarget,
            objectiveOpen: false),
        "a closed objective still hands the whole Army to a new target");

    var followerRecovery = new ArmyFollowerStallRecoveryState();
    for (int i = 0; i < 5; i++)
        Equal(ArmyFollowerStallRecoveryAction.None,
            ArmyFollowerStallRecoveryRules.Observe(followerRecovery,
                actorId: 17L, positionX: 40d, positionY: 12d,
                recoveryEligible: true, combatActive: false,
                transportActive: false),
            "a stationary follower remains on normal recovery before the long-stall timeout");
    Equal(ArmyFollowerStallRecoveryAction.ResetRoute,
        ArmyFollowerStallRecoveryRules.Observe(followerRecovery,
            actorId: 17L, positionX: 40d, positionY: 12d,
            recoveryEligible: true, combatActive: false,
            transportActive: false),
        "a stationary follower first receives a route reset");
    for (int i = 0;
         i < ArmyFollowerStallRecoveryRules.TeleportAfterSeconds - 6;
         i++)
        Equal(ArmyFollowerStallRecoveryAction.None,
            ArmyFollowerStallRecoveryRules.Observe(followerRecovery,
                actorId: 17L, positionX: 40d, positionY: 12d,
                recoveryEligible: true, combatActive: false,
                transportActive: false),
            "the fallback waits until the complete long-stall window elapses");
    Equal(ArmyFollowerStallRecoveryAction.TeleportToCaptain,
        ArmyFollowerStallRecoveryRules.Observe(followerRecovery,
            actorId: 17L, positionX: 40d, positionY: 12d,
            recoveryEligible: true, combatActive: false,
            transportActive: false),
        "a normal follower that remains stranded after route recovery returns to its captain");

    True(ArmyFormationRules.TryGetFallbackRecoveryOffset(0,
            out ArmyFormationOffset firstFallbackRecoveryOffset),
        "a stranded follower has a bounded fallback recovery position when its assigned slot is blocked");
    Equal(false, firstFallbackRecoveryOffset.X == 0 &&
                 firstFallbackRecoveryOffset.Y == 0,
        "a fallback recovery position never overlaps the captain tile");
    Equal(false, ArmyFormationRules.TryGetFallbackRecoveryOffset(-1,
            out _),
        "a negative fallback recovery attempt is rejected");
    Equal(false, ArmyFormationRules.TryGetFallbackRecoveryOffset(
            ArmyFormationRules.MaximumFallbackRecoveryAttempts,
            out _),
        "fallback recovery remains bounded even when no nearby tile is usable");

    var concurrentFollowerRecovery = new ArmyFollowerStallRecoveryIndex();
    Equal(ArmyFollowerStallRecoveryAction.None,
        concurrentFollowerRecovery.Observe(actorId: 21L,
            positionX: 11d, positionY: 12d, recoveryEligible: true,
            combatActive: false, transportActive: false,
            observedRealtime: 100d),
        "the first stranded follower starts its own wall-clock recovery");
    Equal(ArmyFollowerStallRecoveryAction.None,
        concurrentFollowerRecovery.Observe(actorId: 22L,
            positionX: 21d, positionY: 22d, recoveryEligible: true,
            combatActive: false, transportActive: false,
            observedRealtime: 100d),
        "a second stranded follower does not overwrite the first recovery state");
    Equal(ArmyFollowerStallRecoveryAction.ResetRoute,
        concurrentFollowerRecovery.Observe(actorId: 21L,
            positionX: 11d, positionY: 12d, recoveryEligible: true,
            combatActive: false, transportActive: false,
            observedRealtime: 105d),
        "each follower resets its own route after five real seconds");
    Equal(ArmyFollowerStallRecoveryAction.ResetRoute,
        concurrentFollowerRecovery.Observe(actorId: 22L,
            positionX: 21d, positionY: 22d, recoveryEligible: true,
            combatActive: false, transportActive: false,
            observedRealtime: 105d),
        "the second follower receives recovery without waiting for the first");
    Equal(ArmyFollowerStallRecoveryAction.TeleportToCaptain,
        concurrentFollowerRecovery.Observe(actorId: 21L,
            positionX: 11d, positionY: 12d, recoveryEligible: true,
            combatActive: false, transportActive: false,
            observedRealtime: 120d),
        "the first follower teleports after its own long-stall window");
    Equal(ArmyFollowerStallRecoveryAction.TeleportToCaptain,
        concurrentFollowerRecovery.Observe(actorId: 22L,
            positionX: 21d, positionY: 22d, recoveryEligible: true,
            combatActive: false, transportActive: false,
            observedRealtime: 120d),
        "the second follower teleports on the same wall-clock deadline");
    True(ArmySharedPathRules.ShouldSubmitIndependentFollowerRecoveryRoute(
            sharedRouteAvailable: false, recoveryTargetAvailable: true,
            combatActive: false, transportActive: false),
        "a stranded follower without the Army shared route receives one full recovery route before teleport fallback");
    Equal(false,
        ArmySharedPathRules.ShouldSubmitIndependentFollowerRecoveryRoute(
            sharedRouteAvailable: true, recoveryTargetAvailable: true,
            combatActive: false, transportActive: false),
        "a valid shared Army route remains the preferred movement source");
    Equal(false,
        ArmySharedPathRules.ShouldSubmitIndependentFollowerRecoveryRoute(
            sharedRouteAvailable: false, recoveryTargetAvailable: true,
            combatActive: true, transportActive: false),
        "a follower in combat never replaces combat ownership with recovery pathing");

    var transportFollowerRecovery = new ArmyFollowerStallRecoveryState();
    for (int i = 0; i < ArmyFollowerStallRecoveryRules.TeleportAfterSeconds + 4;
         i++)
        Equal(ArmyFollowerStallRecoveryAction.None,
            ArmyFollowerStallRecoveryRules.Observe(transportFollowerRecovery,
                actorId: 18L, positionX: 40d, positionY: 12d,
                recoveryEligible: true, combatActive: false,
                transportActive: true),
            "a passenger or queued transport follower never uses the land teleport fallback");

    var combatFollowerRecovery = new ArmyFollowerStallRecoveryState();
    for (int i = 0; i < ArmyFollowerStallRecoveryRules.TeleportAfterSeconds + 4;
         i++)
        Equal(ArmyFollowerStallRecoveryAction.None,
            ArmyFollowerStallRecoveryRules.Observe(combatFollowerRecovery,
                actorId: 19L, positionX: 40d, positionY: 12d,
                recoveryEligible: true, combatActive: true,
                transportActive: false),
            "a follower in combat never uses the formation teleport fallback");

    var transportOwnedFlow = new ArmyWatchdogRecoveryFlow();
    transportOwnedFlow.AssignMission();
    for (int i = 0; i < 3; i++)
        transportOwnedFlow.ObserveSample(true,
            ArmyWatchdogPositionSource.Captain, 0d, 0,
            routeReady: true, commandExpected: true,
            commandOwned: true);
    transportOwnedFlow.SuspendForExternalOwnership();
    Equal(0d, transportOwnedFlow.StallState.NoProgressSeconds,
        "an active transport voyage clears generic march stall debt");
    for (int i = 0; i < 2; i++)
        Equal(ArmyStallRecoveryAction.None,
            transportOwnedFlow.ObserveSample(true,
                ArmyWatchdogPositionSource.Captain, 0d, 0,
                routeReady: true, commandExpected: true,
                commandOwned: true),
            "generic watchdog restarts its bounded window after landing");

    Equal(25d, ArmyStallWatchdogRules.ResolveObjectiveProgress(
            ArmyRtsObjectiveState.OpenAttack, captureTicks: 25d),
        "attack progress follows the target capture percentage");
    Equal(75d, ArmyStallWatchdogRules.ResolveObjectiveProgress(
            ArmyRtsObjectiveState.OpenDefense, captureTicks: 25d),
        "defense progress increases while hostile capture is reversed");
    var assaultObjectiveFlow = new ArmyWatchdogRecoveryFlow();
    assaultObjectiveFlow.AssignMission();
    for (int i = 0; i < 2; i++)
        Equal(ArmyStallRecoveryAction.None,
            assaultObjectiveFlow.ObserveSample(true,
                ArmyWatchdogPositionSource.Captain, 0d, 0,
                routeReady: false, routePending: false,
                commandExpected: false, commandOwned: true,
                objectiveProgressExpected: true,
                objectiveProgress: 25d),
            "an assault objective receives a bounded progress window");
    Equal(ArmyStallRecoveryAction.ReassertCommand,
        assaultObjectiveFlow.ObserveSample(true,
            ArmyWatchdogPositionSource.Captain, 0d, 0,
            routeReady: false, routePending: false,
            commandExpected: false, commandOwned: true,
            objectiveProgressExpected: true,
            objectiveProgress: 25d),
        "stalled capture progress reasserts the assault command");
    Equal(ArmyStallRecoveryAction.None,
        assaultObjectiveFlow.ObserveSample(true,
            ArmyWatchdogPositionSource.Captain, 0d, 0,
            routeReady: false, routePending: false,
            commandExpected: false, commandOwned: true,
            objectiveProgressExpected: true,
            objectiveProgress: 26d),
        "advancing capture progress clears the assault stall window");
    Equal(0d, assaultObjectiveFlow.StallState.NoProgressSeconds,
        "capture progress clears accumulated no-progress time");

    var combatFlow = new ArmyWatchdogRecoveryFlow();
    combatFlow.AssignMission();
    for (int i = 0; i < 6; i++)
        Equal(ArmyStallRecoveryAction.None,
            combatFlow.ObserveSample(true,
                ArmyWatchdogPositionSource.Captain, 0d, 0,
                routeReady: false, routePending: false,
                commandExpected: false, commandOwned: false),
            "active immediate combat suppresses movement stall recovery");
}

static void Near(float expected, float actual, float tolerance, string name)
{
    if (MathF.Abs(expected - actual) > tolerance)
        throw new InvalidOperationException($"{name}: expected {expected}, got {actual}");
}

static void SequenceEqual<T>(IReadOnlyList<T> expected, IReadOnlyList<T> actual,
    string name)
{
    if (expected.Count != actual.Count)
        throw new InvalidOperationException(
            $"{name}: expected {expected.Count} items, got {actual.Count}");
    for (int i = 0; i < expected.Count; i++)
        if (!EqualityComparer<T>.Default.Equals(expected[i], actual[i]))
            throw new InvalidOperationException(
                $"{name}: item {i} expected {expected[i]}, got {actual[i]}");
}

static CourtManualCandidateFacts CourtFacts(bool alive = true, bool adult = true,
    bool domestic = true, bool slave = false, bool madness = false,
    bool male = true, bool royalAsylum = false, bool king = false,
    bool hasCentralOffice = false, bool affiliationAvailable = true)
{
    return new CourtManualCandidateFacts(alive, adult, domestic, slave, madness,
        male, royalAsylum, king, hasCentralOffice, affiliationAvailable);
}

    "an adult male Mandate dynast can become a feudatory prince");

    "ordinary kingdoms cannot create feudatories");

    "the emperor cannot become a feudatory prince");

    "the current heir remains in the capital");

    "feudatory princes must be male");

    "feudatory princes must be adults");

    "an actor cannot lead two feudatories");

    "great enfeoffment has a bounded prince batch");

    "a zone fallback does not replace a visible city center");

Equal(false, FeudatoryRules.ShouldRunAnnualWork(101, 12, 4),
    "non-matching years do no feudatory work");

    FeudatoryRules.ResolveCityTransfer(false, false, 3, 3,

var feudatorySuccessionCandidates = new[]
{
    new FeudatorySuccessionCandidate(20, eligible: true, directSon: false,
        sameShiBranch: true, kinDistance: 1, birthTime: 20),

        sameShiBranch: true, kinDistance: 1, birthTime: 11),

        sameShiBranch: true, kinDistance: 1, birthTime: 10)

        feudatorySuccessionCandidates),
    "the eldest eligible direct son inherits the whole feudatory");

{

        sameShiBranch: true, kinDistance: 2, birthTime: 15),

        parent1ActorId: 10, parent2ActorId: 20,

        isMandateKingdom: true, alreadyEnabled: false,

        enabled: true, cityCount: 3),

    FeudatoryFavorRules.ResolveSuccessionEffect(

Equal(12L, FeudatoryFavorRules.SelectReclaimedCity(10, new[]
    {
        new FeudatoryFavorCityCandidate(10, distanceToCapital: 1),

    "single-seat favor order autonomy reduction clamps at zero");
Equal(45, FeudatoryFavorRules.ReduceAutonomy(60),
    "single-seat favor order reduces autonomy by fifteen");
Equal(70, FeudatoryAutonomyRules.ApplyCap(
        autonomy: 90, autonomyCap: 70),
    "higher centralization immediately clamps excessive feudatory autonomy");
Equal(40, FeudatoryAutonomyRules.ApplyCap(
        autonomy: 40, autonomyCap: 70),
    "centralization does not raise a feudatory already below the cap");

        autonomy: 80, autonomyCap: 50),

        autonomy: 50, mandateValue: 39),

    new FeudatoryCityCandidate(11, true, 40, new long[] { 10 }),
    new FeudatoryCityCandidate(12, true, 80, new long[] { 9 }),

    totalTechCount: 10, distanceFromCapital: 0f, slavePopulation: 0,
    nonCore: true, activeFief: false, taxMultiplier: 1.05f,
    manpowerMultiplier: 1.05f, unrestReduction: 3f);
var economyLevelThree = CityEconomyRules.CalculateContribution(
    CityEconomyRole.OccupiedUnrest, population: 100, adoptedTechCount: 5,
    totalTechCount: 10, distanceFromCapital: 0f, slavePopulation: 0,
    nonCore: true, activeFief: false, taxMultiplier: 1.10f,
    manpowerMultiplier: 1.10f, unrestReduction: 6f);
Near(economyLevelZero.TaxValue * 1.05f, economyLevelTwo.TaxValue, 0.0001f,
    "level two centralization raises final city tax by five percent");
Near(economyLevelZero.Manpower * 1.05f, economyLevelTwo.Manpower, 0.0001f,
    "level two centralization raises final city manpower by five percent");
Near(economyLevelZero.UnrestRisk - 3f, economyLevelTwo.UnrestRisk, 0.0001f,
    "level two centralization reduces final city unrest by three");
Near(economyLevelZero.TaxValue * 1.10f, economyLevelThree.TaxValue, 0.0001f,
    "level three centralization raises final city tax by ten percent");
Near(economyLevelZero.Manpower * 1.10f, economyLevelThree.Manpower, 0.0001f,
    "level three centralization raises final city manpower by ten percent");
Near(economyLevelZero.UnrestRisk - 6f, economyLevelThree.UnrestRisk, 0.0001f,
    "level three centralization reduces final city unrest by six");
Near(0f, CityEconomyRules.CalculateContribution(
        CityEconomyRole.CapitalAdmin, 0, 0, 0, 0f, 0, false,
        unrestReduction: 999f).UnrestRisk, 0.0001f,
    "centralization cannot reduce final unrest below zero");
Near(100f, CityEconomyRules.CalculateContribution(
        CityEconomyRole.OccupiedUnrest, 0, 0, 0, 0f, 0, true,
        activeFief: true, unrestReduction: -999f).UnrestRisk, 0.0001f,
    "final unrest remains clamped at one hundred");

    CityEconomyRole.AgrarianGranary, 100, 2, 10, 20f, 0, false);

    0.0001f, "feudatory remittance does not fake central manpower loss");

    "subjects of the same root share one calendar sphere");

    "a suzerain does not gain reverse calendar opinion of its subject");

        new RitualDiplomacyFacts(5, 5, -1, 5, 0, 3, false),

var noticeQueueItem = new KingdomDecisionQueueItem
{
    decision_id = "aw_decision_declare_war",
    progress = 100f,
    target_kingdom_id = 22,
    war_goal_type = "force_vassal",
    notice_signature = "11:22:force_vassal:33:100",
    notice_year = 100,
    earliest_war_year = 101,
    forced_war_year = 103,
    notice_recorded = true
};
string encodedNoticeQueue = KingdomDecisionQueueCodec.Encode(new List<KingdomDecisionQueueItem>
{
    noticeQueueItem
});
List<KingdomDecisionQueueItem> decodedNoticeQueue = KingdomDecisionQueueCodec.Decode(encodedNoticeQueue);
Equal(1, decodedNoticeQueue.Count,
    "notice-bearing decision queue row round-trips");

    "notice signature survives decision preemption");

    "notice issue year survives decision preemption");

    "notice earliest year survives decision preemption");

    "notice forced year survives decision preemption");
True(decodedNoticeQueue[0].notice_recorded,
    "notice history deduplication flag survives decision preemption");

{
    decision_id = "aw_decision_appease_xia_cities",
    progress = 27.5f,
    target_kingdom_id = 31,
    target_kingdom_name = "legacy target"
};
string legacyAppeasementQueue = KingdomDecisionQueueCodec.Encode(
    new List<KingdomDecisionQueueItem> { legacyAppeasementQueueItem });
string migratedAppeasementQueue =
    KingdomDecisionQueueCodec.MigrateDecisionIds(
        legacyAppeasementQueue,
        KingdomPolicyProfileMigrationRules.MapLegacyDecisionId,
        out bool replacedLegacyAppeasement);

    KingdomDecisionQueueCodec.Decode(migratedAppeasementQueue);
True(replacedLegacyAppeasement,
    "legacy appeasement queue migration reports replacement");
Equal(1, migratedAppeasementItems.Count,
    "legacy appeasement queue keeps its item");
Equal("aw_decision_appease_foreign_cities",
    migratedAppeasementItems[0].decision_id,
    "legacy appeasement queue maps to the generalized id");
Equal(27.5f, migratedAppeasementItems[0].progress,
    "legacy appeasement queue preserves progress");
Equal(31L, migratedAppeasementItems[0].target_kingdom_id,
    "legacy appeasement queue preserves target metadata");
Equal(legacyAppeasementQueue,
    KingdomDecisionQueueCodec.MigrateDecisionIds(
        legacyAppeasementQueue, id => id,
        out bool replacedUnchangedDecision),
    "unchanged queue migration preserves its exact serialized form");
Equal(false, replacedUnchangedDecision,
    "unchanged queue migration reports no replacement");

var executableAvailabilityConstructor = typeof(DiplomacyAvailabilityFacts)
    .GetConstructor(new[]
    {
        typeof(bool), typeof(bool), typeof(bool), typeof(bool), typeof(bool),
        typeof(bool), typeof(bool), typeof(bool), typeof(bool), typeof(string),
        typeof(string)
    });
True(executableAvailabilityConstructor != null,
    "diplomacy availability carries the live subject and alliance execution failures");
if (executableAvailabilityConstructor != null)
{
    var blockedVassalFacts = (DiplomacyAvailabilityFacts)
        executableAvailabilityConstructor.Invoke(new object[]
        {
            false, false, false, false, false, false, true, false, false,
            "title_too_low", ""
        });
    Equal("title_too_low", DiplomacyProposalRules.UnavailableReason(
            DiplomacyProposalType.Vassalize, blockedVassalFacts),
        "a vassal request blocked by the live title rules is not offered for acceptance");
    Equal("title_too_low", DiplomacyProposalRules.UnavailableReason(
            DiplomacyProposalType.Tributary, blockedVassalFacts),
        "a tributary request uses the same executable subject preflight");

    var blockedAllianceFacts = (DiplomacyAvailabilityFacts)
        executableAvailabilityConstructor.Invoke(new object[]
        {
            false, false, false, false, false, false, false, false, false,
            "", "alliance_conflict"
        });
    Equal("alliance_conflict", DiplomacyProposalRules.UnavailableReason(
            DiplomacyProposalType.Alliance, blockedAllianceFacts),
        "realms in incompatible alliances cannot create an unexecutable proposal");
}

        definition.Char[0]);

        "posthumous meaning locale exists for " + definition.Char);
}
Equal(6, MandateDecisionAiRules.CooldownYears(
        "aw_mandate_decision_border_defense"),
    "border defense has an independent six-year cooldown");
Equal(12, MandateDecisionAiRules.CooldownYears(
        "aw_mandate_decision_great_enfeoffment"),
    "great enfeoffment has an independent twelve-year cooldown");
Equal(false, MandateDecisionAiRules.IsCooldownReady(
        pCurrentYear: 25, pLastSuccessYear: 20, pCooldownYears: 6),
    "repeatable mandate work cannot restart before its own cooldown");
True(MandateDecisionAiRules.IsCooldownReady(
        pCurrentYear: 26, pLastSuccessYear: 20, pCooldownYears: 6),
    "repeatable mandate work becomes available at the cooldown boundary");
True(MandateDecisionAiRules.Score(
        "aw_mandate_decision_great_enfeoffment", false) >
     MandateDecisionAiRules.Score(
        "aw_mandate_decision_border_defense", false),
    "great enfeoffment outranks routine border work");
True(MandateDecisionAiRules.Score(
        "aw_mandate_decision_grant_royal_titles", false) >
     MandateDecisionAiRules.Score(
        "aw_mandate_decision_border_defense", false),
    "great royal grants cannot be starved by routine border work");
True(MandateDecisionAiRules.Score(
        "aw_mandate_decision_sacrifice_moderate", true) >
     MandateDecisionAiRules.Score(
        "aw_mandate_decision_centralize_1", false),
    "the specifically preferred sacrifice remains the highest ritual priority");

    "annual kingdom work is split across sixteen bounded frame slices");

    KingdomAnnualWorkRules.NextStage(KingdomAnnualWorkStage.Succession),
    "succession advances to the separate royal asylum slice");

    KingdomAnnualWorkRules.NextStage(KingdomAnnualWorkStage.RoyalAsylum),
    "royal asylum advances to the separate mobilization slice");

    KingdomAnnualWorkRules.NextStage(KingdomAnnualWorkStage.WarMobilization),
    "mobilization advances to policy");

    "court support advances to auxiliary court work");

    KingdomAnnualWorkRules.NextStage(KingdomAnnualWorkStage.CourtAuxiliary),
    "auxiliary court work advances to posthumous grants");

    KingdomAnnualWorkRules.NextStage(
        KingdomAnnualWorkStage.ConferredPosthumous),
    "posthumous grants advance to diplomatic marriage");

    KingdomAnnualWorkRules.NextStage(
        KingdomAnnualWorkStage.DiplomaticMarriage),
    "diplomatic marriage advances to noble remarriage");
Equal(KingdomAnnualWorkStage.DiplomaticOperation,
    KingdomAnnualWorkRules.NextStage(
        KingdomAnnualWorkStage.NobleRemarriage),
    "noble remarriage advances to diplomatic operations");

    KingdomAnnualWorkRules.NextStage(
        KingdomAnnualWorkStage.DiplomaticOperation),
    "diplomatic operations advance to state economy");

    KingdomAnnualWorkRules.NextStage(KingdomAnnualWorkStage.StateEconomy),
    "economy advances to government maintenance");

    "diplomacy strategy advances to military strategy");

    KingdomAnnualWorkRules.NextStage(KingdomAnnualWorkStage.StrategyMilitary),
    "military strategy completes the annual queue item");

        pendingYear: -1, requestedYear: 75),
    "a kingdom with no pending work accepts its annual request");

        pendingYear: 75, requestedYear: 75),
    "the same kingdom and year are coalesced");

        pendingYear: 75, requestedYear: 76),
    "a newer year supersedes a still-pending annual request");

    "coalescing retains the newest requested year");
Equal("kingdom_annual:42", KingdomAnnualWorkRules.CoalescingKey(42),
    "annual work has one stable queue key per kingdom");

        pActorLastRewardYear: 27);

    "a successful grant requests cooldown writes");

    "a successful grant advances the kingdom cooldown");
Equal(40, committedRewardCooldown.ActorLastRewardYear,
    "a successful grant advances the actor cooldown");

var coalitionAction = new DiplomacyProposalAiCandidate(
    DiplomacyProposalType.Coalition, allowed: true, opinion: 35,
    requesterPowerRatio: 1f, directRoyalMarriage: false,
    targetPowerRatio: 2.2f, targetHasMandate: false, targetKingdomId: 30);
var allianceAction = new DiplomacyProposalAiCandidate(
    DiplomacyProposalType.Alliance, allowed: true, opinion: 75,
    requesterPowerRatio: 1f, directRoyalMarriage: false,
    targetPowerRatio: 0f, targetHasMandate: false);
var equalOpinionAllianceAction = new DiplomacyProposalAiCandidate(
    DiplomacyProposalType.Alliance, allowed: true, opinion: 60,
    requesterPowerRatio: 1f, directRoyalMarriage: false,
    targetPowerRatio: 0f, targetHasMandate: false);
var urgentHouseholdAction = new DiplomacyProposalAiCandidate(
    DiplomacyProposalType.HouseholdOffering, allowed: true, opinion: 60,
    requesterPowerRatio: 1f, directRoyalMarriage: false,
    targetPowerRatio: 0f, targetHasMandate: false, urgency: 30);
var routineHouseholdAction = new DiplomacyProposalAiCandidate(
    DiplomacyProposalType.HouseholdOffering, allowed: true, opinion: 60,
    requesterPowerRatio: 1f, directRoyalMarriage: false,
    targetPowerRatio: 0f, targetHasMandate: false, urgency: 0);

     DiplomacyProposalAiRules.Score(equalOpinionAllianceAction),
    "an empty ruler household outranks a routine alliance");
True(DiplomacyProposalAiRules.Score(routineHouseholdAction) <=
     DiplomacyProposalAiRules.Score(equalOpinionAllianceAction),
    "a populated household does not dominate routine diplomacy");
Equal(DiplomacyProposalType.Coalition,
    DiplomacyProposalAiRules.SelectBest(
        new[] { allianceAction, coalitionAction }).Type,
    "a credible shared threat makes coalition reachable ahead of routine alliance");
var directMarriageAction = new DiplomacyProposalAiCandidate(
    DiplomacyProposalType.RoyalMarriage, allowed: true, opinion: 55,
    requesterPowerRatio: 1f, directRoyalMarriage: true,
    targetPowerRatio: 0f, targetHasMandate: false);
True(DiplomacyProposalAiRules.Score(directMarriageAction) >
     DiplomacyProposalAiRules.Score(allianceAction),
    "a direct royal marriage can outrank a routine alliance");

var foreignCaptainLeaseRule = typeof(ArmyCaptainContinuityRules)
    .GetMethod("ShouldReleaseForeignCaptainLease", new[]
    {
        typeof(bool), typeof(bool), typeof(bool), typeof(bool)
    });
True(foreignCaptainLeaseRule != null,
    "captain continuity exposes a foreign-kingdom lease release rule");
if (foreignCaptainLeaseRule != null)
{
    True((bool)foreignCaptainLeaseRule.Invoke(null,
            new object[] { true, true, false, false }),
        "a living captain who changed kingdoms releases the old army");
    Equal(false, (bool)foreignCaptainLeaseRule.Invoke(null,
            new object[] { true, true, true, false }),
        "a living domestic captain keeps the old army");
    Equal(false, (bool)foreignCaptainLeaseRule.Invoke(null,
            new object[] { true, true, false, true }),
        "army disposal owns its own captain cleanup");
}

var standingMembershipRule = typeof(StandingArmyRules)
    .GetMethod("ShouldEnsureArmyMembership", new[]
    {
        typeof(bool), typeof(bool)
    });
True(standingMembershipRule != null,
    "standing-army rules expose immediate membership repair");
if (standingMembershipRule != null)
{
    True((bool)standingMembershipRule.Invoke(null,
            new object[] { true, false }),
        "a newly appointed warrior without an army is repaired immediately");
    Equal(false, (bool)standingMembershipRule.Invoke(null,
            new object[] { true, true }),
        "an already assigned standing soldier is not reassigned");
}

var zeroArmyRecoveryRule = typeof(TemporaryLevyRules)
    .GetMethod("ShouldRequestZeroArmyRecovery", new[]
    {
        typeof(bool), typeof(int), typeof(bool)
    });
True(zeroArmyRecoveryRule != null,
    "wartime levy rules expose zero-field-army recovery");
if (zeroArmyRecoveryRule != null)
{
    True((bool)zeroArmyRecoveryRule.Invoke(null,
            new object[] { true, 0, false }),
        "wartime zero-army state requests forced recruitment");
    Equal(false, (bool)zeroArmyRecoveryRule.Invoke(null,
            new object[] { true, 1, false }),
        "one usable field army suppresses zero-army recovery");
    Equal(false, (bool)zeroArmyRecoveryRule.Invoke(null,
            new object[] { true, 0, true }),
        "an active recovery plan suppresses duplicate requests");
}

var recruitmentPriorityRule = typeof(TemporaryLevyRules)
    .GetMethod("RecruitmentPriority", new[]
    {
        typeof(bool), typeof(bool)
    });
True(recruitmentPriorityRule != null,
    "wartime levy rules expose deterministic slave and reserve priority");
if (recruitmentPriorityRule != null)
{
    int slavePriority = (int)recruitmentPriorityRule.Invoke(null,
        new object[] { true, false });
    int reservePriority = (int)recruitmentPriorityRule.Invoke(null,
        new object[] { false, true });
    int civilianPriority = (int)recruitmentPriorityRule.Invoke(null,
        new object[] { false, false });
    True(slavePriority < reservePriority,
        "enslaved adults reinforce field armies before retired reserves");
    True(reservePriority < civilianPriority,
        "retired reserves are recruited before ordinary eligible adults");
}
